# JobHunter Traceability Matrix

> English is authoritative. This matrix belongs to [Progress](../progress.md), not another document category or design authority. Snapshot: 2026-09-19, W7 historical review retained with later user-approved CG01-BC1 architecture/plan writeback. The original W5 source baseline is preserved with explicit scoped supersession. It preserves original design-to-document mappings and adds M1 normative readiness and backend implementation evidence in sections 6–7.

> **Current source-model applicability:** CG03S1 at 2026-09-24.S2M1S1-r2 supersedes earlier shared authority, import, Fit and application-allocation mappings only within its scope. See [§6.7](#67-sl-02m1-supplement-reviewed-scope) for the effective decision map and implementation transfer; original mappings/evidence below remain historical, not competing requirements.

## 1. Reading and evidence baseline

[Progress recording rules](README.md) owns traceability/status discipline. The [Decision Register](../design/grill-me-design-tree.md) and its detailed modules preserve provenance; the [English authoring coverage index](../../.scratch/document-authoring-spec.en.md#d-decision-coverage-index) supplies the complete existing-record index. Later scoped corrections control even when an earlier record remains ACCEPTED. The short topic phrases below are locators for effective clauses and excluded historical readings, not replacement requirement prose.

P links identify Product behavior/scope; A links identify Architecture mechanisms or Contract responsibility planning; D links identify Development process obligations. V links identify required proof or an explicit exclusion/pending-detail section in Acceptance. These roles explain why one row can link several documents without assigning the same fact to competing authorities. The [W5 handoff](../../.scratch/w5-progress-handoff.md) preserves original scoped review evidence and now has a revised W6 planning entry; neither is product-test evidence. The [user-approved process revision](../../.scratch/document-authoring-spec.en.md#f-user-approved-delivery-process-revision) adds independent Implementation Plan as the seventh category, assigns W6 planning/W7 joint review and replaces whole-system Contract ordering with progressive normative scope. The 2026-09-19 CS5 refinement retains twelve macro Slices and allows internal milestones to independently complete required Contracts and enter development; other milestones in the same Slice may remain pending. Parent completion requires all required milestones and integrated proof. Its authority is the later user instruction, not a new Q/S record.

The following baseline applies to **every row**, including rows whose accepted clauses have no capability implementation status:

| Mapping element | Current value and limit, preserving original W5 source coverage |
| --- | --- |
| Formal document state | Six main drafts now exist, including [Implementation Plan](../plans/implementation-plan.md); W6 scoped delivery is recorded in its handoff. W7 joint document review is recorded in the [W7 handoff](../../.scratch/w7-joint-review-handoff.md); user baseline approval remains pending |
| W6 planning mappings | [Implementation Plan](../plans/implementation-plan.md) retains twelve macro Slices with 24 internal milestones, exact planned document portions, joint agreements, dependencies and proof. Architecture owns high-level boundaries; [Contract Structure](../contracts/structure.md) carries the agreed file organization preserved from the [structural handoff](../../.scratch/contract-structure-grill-handoff.md); plan allocations have completed W7 document review and await user approval. No normative IDs or implementation are implied |
| Contract requirement ID | COM-001–032, WSP-001–006, MAE-001–018 and STO-001–013 now define M1; later scopes remain Pending. Rejected alternatives/source history do not receive speculative IDs |
| Contract responsibility destination | [Architecture 16](../architecture.md#16-contract-document-structure-and-responsibility-plan) retains high-level families and seven boundary agreements; [Contract Structure](../contracts/structure.md) records the 28 planned documents, file-level mapping/references and structural consumer locators. Normative detail remains pending. Inventory examples are non-normative |
| Implementation location | M1/M2 backend in `backend/src/jobhunter/`; M1 frontend in `frontend/src/`; later capabilities remain pending |
| Executable test/Eval location | Backend tests/fixtures in `backend/tests/`; M1 unit/component/Chromium tests in `frontend/tests/`; other Acceptance destinations remain future proof |
| Executed verification/evidence | Backend checks in sections 7–8; M1 frontend/browser evidence below; no model Eval evidence. Authoring checks alone cannot support implementation |
| Current capability status | M1 and SL-01 Partial; later scope Planned. Original broad source-topic rows are supplemented by the scoped evidence in section 7 |

The dash in a disposition means an excluded alternative, process boundary, research obligation or source history has no capability implementation label; it is not a fifth implementation status. “Pending” describes missing detail or evidence, not a new Domain state. Deferral does not follow merely from missing code. The only repeated primary IDs below are Q23, Q117 and Q175, each explicitly split by clause so that active rules and postponed work do not share a misleading status.

As each internal milestone's required Contract scope is completed, reference its actual stable requirement IDs and split these topic groups where needed to map requirements to implementation, tests/Eval, evidence and status. Use the agreed document-level mapping in [Contract Structure](../contracts/structure.md), its preserved structural provenance and the current owning Slice's milestone locators. Planning identifiers are not Contract IDs. Pending later scope may coexist with completed requirements and does not justify a whole-family completion claim. Do not mint IDs from Q/S numbers, copy target prose into status cells or use a document link as execution proof. Later Slice updates and unified checks follow [Progress recording rules](README.md); no check script is supplied by W5.

### 2.1 Later user change: CG01-BC1

[CG01-BC1](../design/contract/sl-01-m1-grill.md#cg01-bc1) controls the Manual Job portions of Q12/Q17/Q44/Q48/Q50 and CG01-Q2–Q5. ManualApplicationEntry is an independent mutable company/role/application-URL record, separate from formal Job lists and all formal analysis/application consumers. Explicit browser opening is not an application fact. The original 179 source records and 73 original topic rows remain provenance; this later decision is not forged into their Q/S numbering.

The current destinations are Product 2/4/5/7/8/12, Architecture 2/3/4/8/16, Acceptance 3/7/14, Development 6, the revised Slice plans and Contract Structure. Formal Job semantics remain with `jobs/jobs-screening.md`; SL-08.M2 is first producer. SL-01.M1 consumes `jobs/manual-application-entries.md`, workspace and applicable common/storage scope. Formal Job human application reports survive; no Manual entry target does. The preserved Inventory/authoring summaries cannot reinstate the superseded path.

### 2.2 Later architecture supersession: CG03-BC3

[BC3/A1–A5](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-bc3) is approved architecture direction, not a new normative Contract scope. Original source rows/IDs and historical files remain; affected clauses now resolve through this replacement.

| Local expression/presentation; manual lineage versus AI support | Product 3/6, Architecture 5/7, Acceptance 4/6 and [Eval acceptance](../acceptance/evaluation.md#31-capability-specific-review) | Scoped AI representation and commands Pending |
| Separate command atomicity and two-stage editor/Import | [Architecture 16.3](../architecture.md#163-required-boundary-reconciliations), [SL-02](../plans/slices/sl-02-saved-authority-materials.md), [SL-04](../plans/slices/sl-04-resume-import.md), Acceptance 4 | Product choice; no technical ban on valid joint creation |

No retired published requirement IDs are invented: affected S2+ bodies have not been published. Existing 105 SL-01 normative requirements/readiness/evidence remain separate. [CG03-Q46–Q50](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q46) maps fixed-set whitespace/pre-merge admission to P3.3/A5.2/V4.1, Item-owned permanent kind/typed versions to P3.1/A3.1/A5.1/V4.1–4.2, and initial empty Profile to P3.1/P3.3/A5.1/V4.1 plus Profile/Workspace/Storage planning. [CG03-Q51–Q55](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q51) maps saved Profile/Evidence/Resume-root/member/Snapshot fields to Product §3.1/3.3–3.4, Architecture §3.1/5.1–5.3 and Acceptance §4.1–4.2. Q55 assigns normative current-baseline-pointer ownership to Evidence/Baseline; optional physical config storage does not make Workspace a fact owner. [CG03-Q56–Q60](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q56) maps server UUIDv4 to A3.2/V4.1; revision-first canonical publication and scoped timestamps to P3.2/A5.2/V4.1; ResumeVersion envelope/no copied Header contact to P3.3/A5.2/V4.1; and new/switched-only Profile freshness to P3.3/A5.2/V4.2. [CG03-Q61–Q65](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q61) maps document settings/aggregate run.text limits to P3.3/A5.2/V4.1, single-Item M1 Knowledge and full replacement to P3.2/A5.2/V4.1 plus SL-02/SL-04 batch-consumer planning, and one-way readable-history lifecycle/repeated no-op to P3.4/A5.3/V4.2. [CG03-Q66–Q70](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q66) maps scalar/Color Picker admission to P3.3/A5.2/V4.1, POST routes/all-write receipts/per-Item Baseline concurrency to P3.2/A5.2/V4.1, and confirmed adjacent default replacement to P3.4/A5.3/V4.2 plus workspace/resumes-grounding/candidate-save planning. [CG03-Q71–Q75](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q71) maps stable ACTIVE list ordering and small Workspace selection revision to P3.4/A5.3/V4.2, scoped receipt namespace/retention/replay admission to P3.2/A5.2/V4.1, and consistent root/current reads to A5.2/V4.1–4.2. These define neither a generic configuration framework nor universal idempotency infrastructure. [CG03-Q76–Q80](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q76) maps exact remove/default preconditions and consistent full Resume list to P3.4/A5.3/V4.2, retained immutable/Baseline reads to A5.2/V4.1–4.2, and strict JSON/raw limits with byte-specific 413 mapping to A5.2/V4.1 plus planned Common/HTTP consumers. No-op Baseline and default-selection results are command-time snapshots, never current-state reconstruction. [CG03-Q91–Q95](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-grill.md#cg03-q91) maps Evidence list/explicit ID/capacity to P3.1/A5.2/V4.1, planned schema-3/relational lineage/JSON content to A13/V4.1/V10 and Storage, and milestone-scope supersession to P3.5/A5.4/A16.3/V4.3 and the global/SL-02/SL-04/SL-07/SL-10 plans. M1 no longer consumes derived-work intent; materials supplies source/currentness/Save-not-output-ready only. M2 owns actual demand/intent and atomic Save extension. Q98 supersedes Q88 and scopes Q74 without moving mutable admission before replay. The Q98 clarification is accepted: missing target during kind lookup returns 404 before receipt even for a previously used key. The later [§6.3 publication review](#63-sl-02m1-reviewed-scope-and-interface-evidence) supplies the actual consumed IDs/readiness; the preceding batch history is provenance, not implementation or runtime proof.

## 4. Review scope and continuation

Original W1–W5 and structure-check results remain in their historical handoffs. W6 adds the planning column above while preserving all six original source/control/owner/proof/disposition columns except truthful availability wording for Q26/Q32. The [W6 handoff](../../.scratch/w6-implementation-plan-handoff.md) reports this delivery's actual checks, including protected-file comparison.

**Decision-to-Document Traceability Seam: PASS for the reviewed documentation baseline.** All 179 records, 73 clause/topic rows and their owner/proof/planning destinations remain. W7 checked effective source topics and all 29 regressions, including partial supersession, exclusions, detailed-Contract deferrals and later user process authority. See the [W7 report](../../.scratch/w7-joint-review-handoff.md) for method, source-module routing and limits; ID counts alone are not semantic proof.

**Cross-Document Semantic Consistency Seam: PASS after documented corrections.** W7 compared current main documents and supporting owners, including all twelve Slice plans, Contract Structure, specialized Eval proof/procedure and Progress recording rules. Milestone scope readiness, separate dependency categories and parent completion agree. The report records clarified parser repair limits, current ownership references and review-state reconciliation.

The user has started SL-01.M1 and approved CG01-BC1. Its architecture/plan writeback is recorded separately from historical W7 review. The M1 Grill is complete through CG01-Q45 and its four consumed normative scopes are reviewed; the development handoff retains original preparation and now records backend delivery, with frontend/browser verification still pending. Other consumers still require their own normative/research/upstream readiness. W7 creates no normative IDs, code or executed acceptance. Each real milestone later updates its own evidence and parent remainder; shared changes recheck earlier consumers.

**Sources:** Q4/Q22/Q26/Q32/S24.2/S37.1/S38.1 for surviving constraints; explicit CS1–CS5 user authority in the [structural handoff](../../.scratch/contract-structure-grill-handoff.md) and [authoring Further Notes F](../../.scratch/document-authoring-spec.en.md#f-user-approved-delivery-process-revision). Planning allocation belongs to [Implementation Plan](../plans/implementation-plan.md); [Development 5](../development.md#5-future-slice-planning-and-test-first-work) requires ready scope before development, while [Progress recording rules](README.md) owns readiness/status/evidence records.

CG01-BC1 scoped document review is recorded in [Progress 5](../progress.md#5-verification-scope-and-next-step); it is not normative readiness or execution evidence.

The current planning layout separates [global direction](../plans/implementation-plan.md) from [Slice details](../plans/slices/README.md). Milestone links below point directly to their single detailed planning location. [docs/index.md](../index.md) and directory READMEs provide navigation; Contract README/index are navigation and Contract Structure is an organization plan, not normative writeback. Parent/child and scope-level readiness remain unchanged.

## 6. Contract normative scope readiness ledger

**File existence does not complete a family; section completion does not complete a document.** Each row is an independently identifiable planning responsibility portion; broad rows must be subdivided further if only part is later completed. Do not assign READY to a broad row while any consumed clause/interface remains unresolved. The ledger is not a frozen section layout, normative ID scheme or Contract schema.

41 rows below are **Ready**. The other 60 rows remain **Pending**, for 101 rows. This current ledger replaces obsolete source-authority/material-join rows and adds narrowly reviewed supplemental interfaces; historical review maps remain below. Readiness may differ within a document and does not establish actual implementation.

Single DeepFit/result/batch and independent optimization consumer scopes remain distinct. Reviewed portrait/shared boundaries do not complete future Fit/optimization/Import/Session protocols. Old independent fact/material-join portions are removed from this current ledger and preserved in historical §§6.3–6.4; deferred application portions remain Pending and excluded from current delivery gates.

### 6.1 SL-01.M1 reviewed scope and interface evidence

Reviewed scope revision: **2026-09-19.M1-r1**, dated 2026-09-19. The user accepted CG01-Q41–Q45 with the controlling Q42/Q45 refinements. The four normative bodies contain 69 stable requirements. Scoped source-to-Contract and cross-owner semantic reviews passed after corrections; [Progress 6](../progress.md#6-sl-01m1-contract-review-and-handoff) records the findings and limits. This is not a Git commit identifier or executed acceptance.

| Owned scope | Exact normative coverage | Remaining same-family scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Common](../contracts/common.md) | COM-001–032: conventions, M1 scalar/error/strict boundary representation | Formal asset/exact-version, invocation and other consumers' additional schemas |
| [Workspace](../contracts/foundation/workspace.md) | WSP-001–006: singleton/first-use semantics, startup admission, navigation and local access | Resume defaults and later consumers |
| [ManualApplicationEntry](../contracts/jobs/manual-application-entries.md) | MAE-001–018: record, validation, commands, reads, HTTP, browser and uncertainty | No unfinished requirement in the selected M1 entry scope; future scope changes need new review |
| [Storage](../contracts/foundation/storage.md) | STO-001–013: placement, owner lock, schema recognition, transactions, receipts, diagnostics, durability/privacy | Immutable assets and Harness recovery/retention/other storage consumers |

| Joint interface | Owner and consumer agreement |
| --- | --- |
| Startup | Workspace WSP-002–004 consumes Storage STO-001/002/005/006/011; no business listener before success, no Workspace identity derived from path |
| Create/replay/delete | Entry MAE-005/007/008/011 consumes Storage STO-003/004/007/010; receipts outlive deleted business rows without retained text |
| Revision/time/read consistency | Entry MAE-006/009–012 consumes Common COM-017/028 and Storage STO-007/009; stale no-op does not bypass admission |
| Errors and unknown commit | Common COM-029/030 shapes Entry MAE-014/016 and Storage STO-008/011; Workspace WSP-006 owns access rejection. Unknown is a command outcome, not Entry state |
| Browser handoff | Entry MAE-012/013 consumes Workspace WSP-005/006; no transaction spans navigation, no usable opener/referrer before external control |
| Generated API/UI boundary | Common COM-025–031 and Entry MAE-015/018 control DTO/OpenAPI/client/form behavior; generators do not become semantic owners |

| BC1; Q2–Q6 scoped supersession | MAE-001; WSP-001/005; formal Job detail stays pending with its actual consumers |

**Cross-Document Semantic Consistency:** Product §3.5, Architecture §5.4/13/16.3, Acceptance §4.3, the global/SL-02 plans and Contract Structure now describe explicit exact demand, PDF plus one long PNG, independent acceptance/readiness/availability and scoped local recovery. Candidate Save remains unchanged in its nine-command surface; existing Evidence/Profile/Resume full readers retain full validation while M2 gains a distinct internal projection. Shared encoder bytes, old command namespaces and retained migration history remain compatible. Older schema literals apply to their original consumers; M2 adds a forward migration from the actual implementation-time head without fixing a number.

| Necessary seam | Reviewed agreement |
| --- | --- |
| Source producer → Materials | PRO-009/EVD-015/RES-016 + MAT-005/006 + STO-038: exact complete Resume/Profile, ordered typed Evidence fields, no unused expression dependency or weakening of Candidate readers |
| Save → actual demand | SAV-017 + MAT-003/004 + DRW-001: no M2 follow-current/Save intent; preserved conditional atomicity for a future consumer |
| Acceptance → durable dispatch | DRW-006–013 + STO-030/031: receipt/Intent plus full disposition, captured limits, concurrency and lost-wakeup recovery |
| Candidate output → publication | MAT-021/022 + DRW-014/015/019 + STO-033–036: renderer has no publication authority; file durability, attempt fence, atomic result fan-out and live-writer safety |
| Receipt/result → reads | DRW-008/021/024 + MAT-025–028 + STO-035/039: original snapshot replay, no implicit generation/repair, terminal result distinct from byte availability |
| Config catalog → startup/capability | MAT-010–013 + WSP-012 + STO-032: versioned immutable descriptors, legal registration despite missing runtime dependencies, integrity failure distinct from executability |
| New consumer → prior M1 | COM-045 + SAV-010/017 + STO-029/038/039: unchanged encoder/prefixes, no altered full-reader semantics or migration rewrites; actual future regression tests remain required |

A bounded independent read-only audit found no new semantic decision blocking publication. It identified three mechanical omissions/ambiguities: descriptor-version advancement, initial defaults versus optional body-limit suggestion, and reuse versus content-error mapping. MAT-010, DRW-020 and STO-035 were corrected; stale Candidate header applicability was also corrected. These findings do not authorize a new renderer or change prior consumer behavior. The follow-up independent review passed both documentary verification seams for the corrected scope; no additional semantic question or actionable issue remained.

**Executed publication checks:** `python3 scripts/check_contract_links.py` passes for 251 unique anchored requirements, all 135 decision mappings, local paths/anchors and the 26 Ready / 63 Pending / 89-row ledger. `.venv/bin/ruff check scripts/check_contract_links.py`, `.venv/bin/ruff format --check scripts/check_contract_links.py` and `.venv/bin/pyright scripts/check_contract_links.py` pass (zero Pyright errors/warnings). Seven isolated negative fixtures reject duplicate/unknown IDs, missing files/anchors, omitted decision mapping, incorrect readiness totals and missing requirement anchors; the restored baseline passes. Supplemental changed-owner link and whitespace checks, including new files, and `git diff --check` pass. The [Grill publication closure](../design/contract/sl-02-m2-grill.md#cg04-pub) preserves the authorization, supersession and evidence boundary. No backend/runtime suite was executed by this publication.

**Remaining implementation/research at publication:** actual catalog UUIDs/font files/hashes/distribution rights; concrete pipeline/installation/isolation; all four font/mark support; Source Han Sans actual synthesis in both formats; template parameters/labels/wrapping; PDF tolerance/text extraction; PNG width; measured operational limits; actual next migration revision and filesystem crash/durability evidence. No code/test mapping is fabricated. The [backend development handoff](../development/handoff/sl-02-m2-handoff.md) records ordered engineering work and the actual M1 source/evidence checkpoint. M1 backend is available as separately recorded in §9, but its frontend and all M2 implementation/integration were unfinished at that checkpoint. Current backend evidence appears below; Contract Ready does not establish executable rendering or parent-Slice completion.

### 6.5 SL-03.M1 reviewed scope and interface evidence

Reviewed revision: **2026-09-23.S3M1-r1**. CG05-Q1–Q130 are accepted with their recorded scoped supersessions; the user explicitly authorized closure review, necessary writeback and normative publication. This release adds **49 requirements**: EXR-001–034, COM-047–048, STO-040–045 and EVO-001–007, bringing actual definitions to **300 across 13 bodies**. Prior 251 IDs and consumer meanings are preserved. Four M1-only portions are Ready; broad future consumer rows remain Pending, giving **30 Ready / 63 Pending / 93 total**. File/family-wide readiness is not inferred.

**Decision-to-Document Traceability: PASS.** Every effective decision below has a formal destination, including explicit deferral requirements. Partial supersessions Q8/Q89 and Q115/Q129, Q45's stable-byte clarification, postdeadline refinement and rejected speculative fields/codes are preserved. Logical internal call/result tables consolidate accepted predicates and projections; they do not create HTTP protocols, public generic state setters or business result authority.

**Cross-Document Semantic Consistency: PASS after scoped corrections.** The register, Product §9.2/9.3, Architecture §12/13, Acceptance §9.2, SL-03/global plans, Common/Workspace/Storage, existing Candidate/Materials boundaries and new Runtime/evidence bodies were checked by responsibility. Product requires no new user-visible behavior or writeback. Architecture and Acceptance now distinguish original-live-path reconciliation from crash replay, read-only historical response confirmation from new publication, finite postdeadline local recovery from new remote effects, and lawful whole-Run ending from bypassing execution authority. Plans/readiness now consume protection-only storage and controlled adapters/Tool proof, keeping real purge and production integration pending. No prior receipt/Materials behavior is replaced.

| Interface / compatibility seam | Reviewed conclusion |
| --- | --- |
| Consumer → Runtime → Gateway | Typed consumer descriptor/exact refs atomically freeze with intent; unique live winner owns at most one send; concrete production formats/admission remain M2 |
| Response producer → Storage → recovery reader | Invocation owns format; deterministic UTF-8 and exact integrity; atomic payload/phase; original generation/path for first publication; read-only identical confirmation remains legal after ending |
| Consumer result/admission → recovery/ending | Whole-Run completion and finite local bounds/denial mappings stay consumer-owned; current qualification or ownerless CAS still arbitrates ending; storage uncertainty is not missing evidence |
| Tool consumer → Runtime | Typed controlled exact-version pure-read proof closes M1; unfinished same-Invocation recovery is distinct from a new Context acquisition; no hidden Ensure or MODEL replay |
| Run → payload protection / schema | All OPEN-Run responses retained, no step-level release; terminal payload retained because M1 has no purge; actual-head forward migration preserves previous consumers |
| Existing Workspace/Entry/Preferences/Candidate/Materials | Reuse physical ownership and exact transaction outcomes; no HTTP/scalar/timestamp/lifecycle/replay migration of earlier consumers into Run semantics |
| Evidence producer → Progress | EVO requirements describe future actual-component tests; no Runtime code, SDK, migration execution or semantic acceptance supplied by this document release |

Two bounded read-only checks verified actual upstream source and critical semantic seams. A subsequent bounded draft check corrected overly narrow OPEN protection and Tool result wording, overly broad draining prohibition, explicit original-path publication checks and completed internal operation shape tables. No unresolved semantic blocker remains for the four published M1 portions. Future consumer mappings/bounds must close before enabling those consumers; they are not blanket M1 implementation evidence.

**Actual upstream checkpoint:** HEAD 1277e2af7180f9765c0542c92c1fb4d1ffb44800; Candidate committed in 2cff572, Materials in current HEAD; Store targets schema 4, migration head a41d7e90c263 after ef03c92ba671. Source composition provides Entry/Preferences/CandidateAuthority/Materials, but no AgentRun/Invocation runtime or Provider SDK. The 330-test backend result in §10 is inherited evidence, not rerun here. Historical Grill-entry/schema-3 and older handoff snapshots are preserved. The [new handoff](../development/handoff/sl-03-m1-handoff.md) carries current source and actual-head migration instructions.

**Executed publication checks:** `python3 scripts/check_contract_links.py` passes for **300 unique anchored requirements**, **2773 local links/anchors**, **135 CG04 / 130 CG05 mappings**, coverage of all 49 new normative destinations and the **30 Ready / 63 Pending / 93-row** ledger. `.venv/bin/ruff check scripts/check_contract_links.py`, `.venv/bin/ruff format --check scripts/check_contract_links.py` and `.venv/bin/pyright scripts/check_contract_links.py` pass (zero errors/warnings). Seven isolated temporary negative fixtures reject duplicate/unknown IDs, a missing requirement anchor, a missing linked file, omitted CG05 mapping, omitted new normative destination and incorrect readiness count; the restored fixture baseline passes. Comparison against HEAD confirms all 251 earlier requirement bodies are unchanged. Changed/new-file whitespace/conflict-marker checks and `git diff --check` pass. No backend/runtime test suite was executed. Contract review is complete only for the published scope; implementation remains Not started and Runtime acceptance Not executed. No backend/frontend implementation, installation, user-data migration, Git commit or push is part of this publication.

### 6.6 SL-03.M2 reviewed scope and interface evidence

Revision **2026-09-24.S3M2-r1** follows CG06-Q1–Q219 with all accepted amendments and the explicit owner-writeback authorization “同意授权”. The [single register](../design/contract/sl-03-m2-grill.md#cg06-pub) preserves history and the accepted **Q217–Q219** exercise amendments. The consumed documentary scope is Ready; implementation, dependency adoption and executed acceptance remain outstanding.

The 105 additions are COM-049, EXR-035–057, CTX-001–015, TOL-001–014, BUD-001–025, STO-046–053 and EVO-008–026. CTX-003 and EVO-022–026 now close typed exercise inputs, strict exercise-only success, owner-preserving failures and the consumer-bound recovery window; EXR-057 scopes the M2 ending extension without changing M1 consumers. M1's 49 requirements and all earlier IDs/clauses remain intact. Future business, interactive, platform and background agreements retain their separate rows below/above.

**Decision-to-Document Traceability:** each of the 219 effective accepted decisions has a row below. Original superseded fragments are not restored: Skill semantic bounds versus resource authority; Run references versus Frame prompt truth; historical confirmation versus fresh permission; action completion versus result admission; response equality versus usage; CNY-only optional Operation money versus no Run money; immutable Trial evidence versus reexecution; and provider-specific formats/continuation versus generic Gateway ownership are retained. Q217–Q219 map to explicit exercise rules, without a generic semantic-quality gate, Runtime-wide validation taxonomy or AgentRun grace field. Representation-only authoring is recorded in CG06-PUB, including COM-045 Package/Frame encoding, closed provenance, read projection and usage shape.

**Cross-Document Semantic Consistency:** scoped Architecture 9–13/15, Acceptance 9.4, Eval acceptance 2.5, the SL-03 plan and Contract navigation agree on the owner boundaries, serial first consumer, optional CNY budget, one production DeepSeek adapter, no hidden retry and current implementation/adoption exclusions. No Product change was needed. Backend source is a compatibility input, not an authority for new semantic policy. Current M1 recorded evidence remains in section 11; this authoring did not rerun its 416-test suite.

| Interface reviewed | Closed foundation / remaining consumer boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Application → semantic-start | Stable start identity, versioned original-request fingerprint, historical confirmation before fresh-create checks, local-authority atomic publication and typed rejection/uncertainty; caller preserves original logical request |
| Skill → Context → Runtime | Exact static/purpose binding; discardable candidate; actual deterministic semantic Frame, attached provenance/shared estimate; serial PREPARED and atomic Frame/resource/intent publication |
| Runtime → Gateway → adapter | Original live path, no transferable send grant, one request, controlled transport, current DeepSeek one-choice terminal format and independent usage/observations; executable profile/wire proof still required |
| MODEL → Tool → next Frame | Durable same-Run request locator, unique TOOL association, exact target/permission, authoritative validator, preserved completion versus result projection; current continuation requires all retained calls' lawful replies |
| Budget → persistence/recovery | Immutable allocation/reservation bases, checked int64 and exact CNY, absolute component settlement, trusted late evidence, no estimated final settlement, durable unresolved exposure/obstruction and safe proven no-send release |
| Evidence → Eval/export | Canonical reference-first retention, actual post-state, independent evaluator attempts/resources, immutable re-evaluation, pre-SDK allowlist, countable Harness observations, bounded best-effort export and correlated non-generation late accounting |
| Whole exercise → Run ending/recovery | EVO-022–026 / EXR-057: exact final predicate, owner-defined failure meanings and consumer-bound finite local recovery; completed facts persist after window expiry. Existing M1 consumers keep their own agreements |

Final closure checks pass: 405 requirements, 219 CG06 mappings, 38 Ready / 62 Pending / 100 scopes, checker Ruff/format/strict Pyright and six isolated negative fixtures. Prior normative bodies remain intact. The initial foundation checkpoint is preserved in CG06. Independent bounded read-only reviews checked the new formats, M1 compatibility and Eval/evidence/export boundaries. Corrections closed nested shape ambiguity, fixture-input overrestriction, evaluator resource retention and preserved LocalRead field mapping. Mechanical results and actual limits are recorded in the publication verification entry; link/ID coverage alone is not semantic proof. No SDK installation, service, migration, live call or feature implementation occurred.

### 6.7 SL-02.M1 supplement reviewed scope

**Revision:** 2026-09-24.S2M1S1-r1. Accepted CG03S1-BC1–BC3 and Q6–Q41 (Q24 fully superseded; Q16 partially superseded; Q29 option A) are closed for this supplement. Q1–Q5 were withdrawn unanswered. The [register](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-supplement-grill.md) preserves the original decision progression; earlier “open/writeback pending” statements inside accepted historical entries are resolved by this publication mapping, not new blockers.

**Post-publication naming correction:** The user-approved [Profile entry naming amendment](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-supplement-grill.md#cg03s1-profile-entry-naming) updates PRO-011–013 to `entries: ProfileIndexEntry[]` and the model-output `entries` key. Field contents, source support and schema v1 remain unchanged; Product/API/handoff expressions agree. No replacement implementation or legacy alias is introduced.

<a id="entry-incremental-review"></a>

**Entry-scoped amendment — 2026-09-24.S2M1S1-r2:** Accepted [Q42–Q46](../design/contract/sl-02-m1-supplement-grill.md#cg03s1-q42) closes the previously reopened consumer interface. Q42 replaces recursive cross-Entry closure with one source_entry_id per ProfileIndexEntry. PRO-011/013 carries that identity and grouped model output; PRO-012 adds truthful INCREMENTAL provenance; PRO-014 scopes the single-request policy to this consumer version. PRO-017–020/EVD-024 define same-Resume compatible baseline selection, exact historical reattachment versus new-version assembly, complete dirty-Entry generation, unchanged-group reuse, deletion and deterministic merge. SAV-020/022/026/STO-059 close mode-aware FULL refresh, frozen plan and recoverable publication; CTX-016/EVO-028 close input and proof. Eight new IDs raise the retained total to 461. No generic Runtime lifecycle or source-authority change is introduced.

**Implementation transfer:** The semantic Contract is Ready; production SL-03.M2 adoption, consumer implementation and Eval are still required. [SL-02.M1](../development/handoff/sl-02-m1-handoff.md) and [SL-03.M2](../development/handoff/sl-03-m2-handoff.md) now carry concrete backend and frontend deltas. Existing schema-6 deterministic source/Evidence/Materials remain independently useful, but its Profile DTO/validators and any-active-build refresh matching need r2 adaptation. Its earlier tests do not prove the new behavior. SL-05.M1/M2 handoffs add Entry attribution and runtime-only aggregation; their Fit-specific Contracts remain Pending. The API guide distinguishes the served deterministic checkpoint from target Profile source_entry_id/INCREMENTAL shapes. No product code, migration, generated client, provider call or reset is changed by this publication.

**r2 Decision-to-Document review:** Q42 maps Entry locality and removal of recursive propagation; Q43 maps baseline/currentness and compatibility; Q44 maps only the current consumer request policy; Q45 confirms no cross-Entry deletion propagation; Q46 maps explicit full regeneration. The explicit-empty group representation, source-order assembly, mixed provenance and frozen-plan/refresh matching are necessary producer/consumer arrangements for those accepted semantics, not independent capability expansion. They preserve the original unsupported-claim rejection, source privacy, no partial publication, exact history and uncertain-no-replay rules. User wording does not introduce a new global career field or automatic model batching.

**r2 Cross-Document review:** Product/Architecture, normative producer/consumer clauses, Acceptance/Eval, Slice plans, actual API checkpoint, current handoffs and this scope ledger agree on Entry locality, same-Resume reuse, model input/output scope, currentness, FULL refresh and durable recovery. Both documentation seams pass after correcting stale whole-source input descriptions, explicit-empty output coverage, FULL-versus-AUTOMATIC dedup and newest-compatible exact-version reattachment after corrective refreshes. Mechanical checks pass: `python3 scripts/check_contract_links.py` reports 461 unique requirements, 5421 local links/anchors, 41 CG03S1 question mappings plus three baselines and unchanged historical CG04/CG05/CG06 coverage; readiness is 41 Ready / 60 Pending / 101 total. Ruff lint/format for the checker and `git diff --check` pass. Earlier r1 publication results below remain historical; runtime/provider/browser acceptance is not inferred from documentation.

**New requirement IDs:** COM-050–051, WSP-013–015, RES-017–024, EVD-016–023, PRO-010–016, SAV-018–025, STO-054–058, MAT-031–033, PRF-024, CTX-016, TOL-015, EVO-027: 48 additions, 453 retained stable IDs. No ID is reused for opposite authority semantics. Current applicability notices in each owner identify supersession; preserved scalar/contact/AST/receipt/transport/render/runtime clauses are explicitly reused.

**Supersession:** Shared Profile/Evidence/Baseline authority/CRUD/seeds, source adoption and joins; last-Resume prohibition; mandatory two-stage import; parallel Fits and full-baseline/no-retrieval policy; current assistant apply/adopt-back; old Candidate request compatibility and legacy-data migration/archive obligations are replaced. Q38/Q41 controls over Q24. Actual post-transition immutable histories, external consent, source privacy, new receipt replay and Runtime no-replay remain required. Earlier §§6.3–6.6 and executed test evidence are historical scoped proof, not acceptance of these new inputs.

**Decision-to-Document review:** Effective decisions map above to current normative owners, Product §§3/5/6/7, Architecture §§3/5/6/7/13/16, Acceptance §§4–6/10 and EVO-027. Reverse review traces every new ID to the accepted source/operation/field/transition or its necessary representation; schema-v1 profile business fields remain Q37’s three values, not an expanded taxonomy. Future Fit/optimization/import/session/approval payload and score policies are explicitly Pending, not silently authored by examples.

**Cross-document review:** Compare independent source/contacts and canonical AST, exact Evidence/source admission, pair/build fence/refresh/empty lifecycle, bounded model recovery, optional intent, frozen DeepFit and selected-only suggestions, direct material source and unchanged consent, reset scope and delayed application across Contracts, main owners, plans, API target guides and all handoffs below. Historical snapshots are visibly superseded. Shared Runtime conformance protocols are unchanged. Both documentary seams pass after resolving stale product entry/Skill/scoring references, missing Common/Materials schema applicability, whole-Run completion ordering, explicit state nullability and restored unaffected parser/wait/material-consent safeguards. `python3 scripts/check_contract_links.py` passes 453 unique requirements, all checked local links, 135 CG04 / 130 CG05 / 219 CG06 / 36 CG03S1 question mappings plus 3 baselines, and 40 Ready / 60 Pending / 100 current rows. Ruff lint/format of the documentation checker and `git diff --check` pass. In-memory negative checks verify rejection of duplicate IDs, bad links, lost supplemental/history mappings and readiness drift. No runtime/browser/provider/migration execution is inferred.

**Actual implementation gap:** At HEAD 66e2bb8/schema 5, source authorities/DTOs/joins/editor are the old model. M1 prior backend/frontend (80 unit/component, 31 Chromium) and M2 renderer/Runtime prior tests establish only their recorded old scope; none were rerun here. Production SL-03.M2 is Ready as a Contract but unimplemented. No product code, generated clients, migrations, reset, dependency install or Git submission accompanies this publication.

No tests were skipped. Final source migration inspection (`uv run --locked alembic -c backend/alembic.ini heads`) reports only `d092ea64bf17`. The 86 added Runtime/storage/process cases supplement the 330 maintained upstream regressions; inherited checkpoints did not substitute for this execution. Final documentary evidence writeback receives link/whitespace checks without another unchanged backend suite run.
